import subprocess, re

fn = "output/images/.a333-pack/pack_out/usbtool.fex"

raw_base = 0x18600
vma_base = 0x1001a000

need = (
    "uboot down and run addr",
    "fes2 size",
    "down fes2 ok",
    "run fes2 ok",
    "fel down fes2 failed",
    "UBOOT_0000000000",
    "try to down and run fes2",
)

out = subprocess.check_output(
    ["strings", "-t", "x", fn],
    text=True,
    errors="replace"
)

for line in out.splitlines():
    if any(s in line for s in need):
        m = re.match(r"\s*([0-9a-fA-F]+)\s+(.*)", line)
        if not m:
            continue
        off = int(m.group(1), 16)
        vma = vma_base + off - raw_base
        print(f"file=0x{off:05x}  VMA=0x{vma:08x}  {m.group(2)}")

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import struct

p = Path("/tmp/u-boot-fes2.fex")
b = bytearray(p.read_bytes())

# Official usbtool patch:
work_mode = struct.unpack_from("<I", b, 0xe0)[0]
struct.pack_into("<I", b, 0xe0, work_mode | 0x10)

# Real DRAM size detected by FES1, in MiB:
struct.pack_into("<I", b, 0x4f8, 2048)

p.write_bytes(b)

print("work_mode      =", hex(struct.unpack_from("<I", b, 0xe0)[0]))
print("dram_scan_size =", struct.unpack_from("<I", b, 0x4f8)[0], "MiB")
PY

dram_scan_size = 2048 MiB

import struct
